### [1] [Quark stars in regularized 4D Einstein-Gauss-Bonnet gravity: A perturbative QCD equation of state](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13227)
*Anirudh Pradhan,Safiqul Islam,Safyan Mukhtar,Santosh Kumar Dixit*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了在正则化四维爱因斯坦–高斯–邦内特（4DEGB）引力框架下，基于微扰QCD物态方程（Fraga–Kurkela–Vuorinen EOS）的自束缚夸克星的平衡结构与稳定性；发现高曲率修正显著提升最大质量，软物态需较大GB耦合α≈10 km²才能满足观测质量下限，而硬物态在广义相对论极限下已超PSR J0952−0607与GW190814次级质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释当前最重脉冲星（如PSR J0952−0607, ~2.35 M⊙）和致密双星并合事件（如GW190814次级质量~2.50–2.67 M⊙）对致密星物态与引力理论的联合约束；检验4DEGB引力能否在不引入bag常数的前提下支持更高质量的夸克星。

Method: 采用标量-张量形式的4DEGB引力导出修正的TOV方程；结合无有效bag常数、仅由压强归零定义表面的微扰QCD物态方程（参数X=3,4）；数值求解恒星结构，分析最大质量、致密度C=M/R、径向声速平方cs²分布及稳定性。

Result: 软物态（X=3）下，最大质量从GR的2.0431 M⊙增至α=10 km²时的2.5013 M⊙，仅后者落入PSR J0952−0607质量区间；硬物态（X=4）在GR下已达3.0415 M⊙，全面超越GW190814次级与PSR J0952−0607质量；所有构型致密度C∈[0.2531,0.3123]，低于Buchdahl极限；cs²<1/3处处成立，保证因果性与亚共形性。

Conclusion: 4DEGB引力中的高阶曲率修正系统性增强微扰QCD夸克星所能支撑的最大质量；硬物态分支在纯GR下已可解释最重观测致密星，软物态则需显著GB耦合（α∼10 km²）；该结果为区分夸克物质模型与修改引力理论提供了新的质量-致密度诊断窗口。

Abstract: We investigate the equilibrium structure and stability of selfbound quark stars in the framework of regularized four-dimensional Einstein--Gauss--Bonnet (4DEGB) gravity, employing the perturbative QCD equation of state of Fraga, Kurkela and Vuorinen [1]. The equation of state, parameterised by a single renormalization-scale parameter $X$, contains no effective bag constant and defines the stellar surface entirely through the vanishing of the quark-matter pressure. We solve the modified Tolman-Oppenheimer--Volkoff equations derived from the scalar--tensor formulation of 4DEGB gravity for $X \in \{3, 4\}$ and Gauss--Bonnet coupling $α\in \{0, 1, 10\}\,\mathrm{km}^2$. For the soft EOS ($X = 3$), the maximum mass increases from $2.0431\,M_\odot$ (GR) to $2.5013\,M_\odot$ at $α= 10\,\mathrm{km}^2$, with only the latter entering the PSR~J0952$-$0607 mass band. For the stiff EOS($X = 4$), the GR baseline already yields $3.0415\,M_\odot$, and all configurations exceed both the GW190814 secondary mass and the PSR~J0952$-$0607 constraint. The compactness $C = M/R$ at maximum mass ranges from $0.2531$ to $0.3123$ across all configurations, remaining well below the Buchdahl bound throughout. Radial profiles of the squared speed of sound confirm that $c_s^2 < 1/3$ holds pointwise throughout the stellar interior for all parameter choices, establishing that the quark matter remains sub-conformal and causal inside the maximum-mass star. These results demonstrate that higher-curvature corrections in 4DEGB gravity systematically enhance the maximum supported mass of perturbative QCD quark stars, with the stiff ($X = 4$) branch already exceeding the most massive compact objects currently observed even at the GR level, while the soft ($X = 3$) branch requires $\alphaGB \sim 10\,\mathrm{km}^2$ to approach those thresholds.

</details>


### [2] [Black Holes, Gravitational Waves and Space-Time Singularities Lemaitre Conference 2024](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13266)
*Massimo Bianchi,Sergio Luigi Cacciatori,Matteo Galaverni,Gabriele Gionti,Fabio Scardigli*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本专题聚焦于勒梅特会议2024的十九篇论文，涵盖宇宙学、引力与量子理论交叉前沿问题，包括哈勃张力、ΛCDM模型挑战、暴胀与暗能量的弦论/超引力构造、Swampland约束、量子宇宙波函数的观测者依赖性、奇点消解（从勒梅特尘埃模型正则量子化到预大爆炸反弹及无奇点黑洞核）、原初黑洞及其引力波信号、背景无关量子引力、视界热力学第一性原理推导，以及引力语境下的量子—经典过渡基础问题；并含月基宇宙学观测提议与勒梅特原始原子假说历史溯源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 延续乔治·勒梅特科学遗产，回应当前宇宙学与基础物理中一系列根本性开放问题，尤其在哈勃张力凸显ΛCDM危机、量子引力缺乏实验锚点、奇点与视界概念亟需概念重构的背景下，重审勒梅特对坐标/物理奇点区分及‘视界’术语的原创贡献。

Method: 集合理论建模（如弦论/超引力中暗能量构造、Lemaitre 1933尘埃模型正则量子化）、有效理论分析（Swampland约束）、量子宇宙学框架（Wheeler–DeWitt方程与观测者依赖诠释）、奇点消解方案比较（预大爆炸、多体黑洞核）、引力波天文学反演（原初黑洞信号提取）、历史文献考据（原始原子假说形成过程）。

Result: 系统梳理了当前解决Hubble tension、奇点物理、量子宇宙波函数诠释、视界热力学起源等关键问题的多种前沿路径；揭示了勒梅特原始思想（如物理奇点判据、视界概念）对当代研究的持续指导价值；提出月基观测作为突破宇宙学系统误差的新范式。

Conclusion: 勒梅特所开创的概念框架——特别是对奇点本质与因果边界的物理判据——仍构成连接经典宇宙学到量子引力的不可或缺的桥梁；本次专题表明，唯有在历史深度与数学严格性双重约束下推进理论建构，方能实质性逼近宇宙学基本定律。

Abstract: This editorial introduces the topical collection arising from the Lemaitre Conference 2024 - the second in a series of meetings dedicated to the scientific legacy of Georges Lemaitre - held at the Vatican Observatory in Castel Gandolfo, 17-20 June 2024, fifty-seven years after Lemaitre's death and on the eve of the centenary of his foundational 1927 paper on the expanding universe. The nineteen contributions collected here address the most pressing open problems at the interface of cosmology, gravitation, and quantum theory: the Hubble tension and the future of $Λ$CDM in light of recent DESI data; inflationary cosmology and dark-energy model-building in string theory and supergravity, together with the Swampland constraints that bound them; the observer-dependence of the quantum-cosmological wave function; the removal, or consistent crossing, of spacetime singularities - from the canonical quantization of Lemaitre's own 1933 dust model to string-theoretic pre-Big-Bang bounces and many-body constructions of singularity-free black-hole cores; primordial black holes and their gravitational-wave signatures; the search for a background-independent formulation of quantum gravity and a first-principles account of horizon thermodynamics; and the foundational status of the quantum-to-classical transition in gravitational contexts. A proposal for lunar-based cosmological observations, and a historical reconstruction of the genesis of Lemaitre's primeval-atom hypothesis, complete the collection. A recurring unifying thread was the legacy of Lemaitre himself - who first distinguished coordinate from physical singularities and coined the term "horizon" - whose pioneering vision continues to guide research at the frontier of cosmology and fundamental physics.

</details>


### [3] [Nonequilibrium Apparent-Horizon Thermodynamics in Effective Loop Quantum Cosmology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13720)
*Xiao Liu*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了有效圈量子宇宙学（LQC）中表观视界热力学一致性问题，发现其偏离经典平衡热力学，需引入内熵产项的非平衡热力学框架描述，并成功恢复经典与德西特极限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验有效圈量子宇宙学中表观视界的热力学自洽性，特别是标准热力学量定义下是否仍满足经典平衡热力学关系。

Method: 在保留标准视界热力学量（如温度、熵）定义的前提下，基于有效LQC修正的动力学方程，推导视界热力学第一定律并分析熵变结构，引入内熵产项构建非平衡热力学表述。

Result: 发现LQC修正导致经典热力学第一定律出现额外熵产项；该内熵产项在普朗克尺度修正消失时退化为零（恢复经典极限），在宇宙常数主导时趋于德西特熵产行为。

Conclusion: 有效LQC中表观视界具有内在非平衡热力学本质，其热力学行为可由含内熵产的非平衡框架一致描述，且自然涵盖经典与德西特极限。

Abstract: In this work, we investigate the thermodynamic consistency of the apparent horizon in effective loop quantum cosmology (LQC). By retaining the standard definitions of horizon thermodynamic quantities, we find that the modified LQC dynamics leads to a departure from the classical equilibrium thermodynamic description. This deviation can be naturally described by a nonequilibrium formulation characterized by an internal entropy production term. The resulting entropy production consistently recovers the classical and de Sitter limits. These results suggest that the apparent horizon in effective LQC admits a coherent nonequilibrium thermodynamic interpretation.

</details>

*Samaneh Sarbaz,Sohrab Rahvar*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文计算了由克尔度规描述的旋转透镜因横向运动引起的引力透镜频移（Δν/ν），旨在为测量透镜横向速度或自旋参数提供新方法，尤其适用于黑洞微透镜观测中自旋-速度简并问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决引力微透镜中透镜自旋参数与横向速度之间的参数简并问题，并利用真实天体物理场景（如恒星演化形成的旋转黑洞及星系中心超大质量黑洞）开展克尔度规下可观测频移效应的检验。

Method: 基于克尔度规，解析计算横向运动旋转透镜所导致的引力透镜相对频率偏移 Δν/ν，对比此前仅使用史瓦西度规的研究。

Result: 给出了克尔透镜横向运动诱导的频移表达式，表明该效应可独立约束透镜自旋参量 a 和横向速度 v_T，从而打破微透镜观测中的简并。

Conclusion: 克尔度规下的横向运动频移效应是一个可观测、有物理意义的新探针，可用于当前和未来高精度微透镜巡天（如LSST、Roman）中识别和刻画旋转黑洞透镜。

Abstract: In this study, we calculate the relative frequency shift ($Δν/ ν$) caused by gravitational lensing resulting from the transverse motion of a spinning lens described by the Kerr metric \cite{Kerr:1963ud}. While the effect of transverse velocity on the frequency shift has previously been investigated using the Schwarzschild metric \cite{Rahvar_2020,10.1093/mnras/stae1938}, extending this to the Kerr metric provides a new window for measuring the transverse velocity of a lens or its spin parameter, a parameter often subject to degeneracy in gravitational microlensing observations. Recent microlensing observations have revealed black holes as viable lensing candidates \cite{2022ApJ...933...83S}. Since spinning black holes form naturally from stellar evolution, astrophysical black hole lenses are best described by the Kerr metric. Furthermore, recent observations of supermassive black holes at the centers of galaxies provide an ideal laboratory for testing the observational effects of the Kerr metric by measuring the frequency shifts of lensed sources.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Result: 1) 获得M、T_H、S、C关于无量纲视界半径r_h/√α的精确解析式，复现对数熵修正与热容发散；2) 确定两个规范系综鞍点：近极值支（局部极小值，C>0）与大黑洞支（局部极大值，C<0）；3) 发现两鞍点在r_h/√α=√[(4+2√7)/3]处合并，对应T_H极大值与C→±∞；4) 明确该发散是Davies型局域稳定性转变，非全局一级相变。

Conclusion: 本文的核心贡献在于为既有的qOS黑洞热力学提供了清晰、自洽的规范系综鞍点组织图景，严格界定了该模型的有效适用域（局域热平衡、需边界数据约束）及其根本局限（无法定义全局渐近平直配分函数）。

Abstract: We study the local canonical thermodynamics of the quantum Oppenheimer--Snyder (qOS) black-hole exterior previously obtained from loop quantum cosmology. The spacetime is adopted from the literature; no new black-hole solution, regular extension, or singularity-resolution mechanism is claimed here. At fixed quantum parameter $α$, we rewrite the outer horizon in a dimensionless form and obtain exact expressions for the mass, Hawking temperature, entropy, and heat capacity. These expressions reproduce the logarithmic entropy term and the heat-capacity divergence reported in earlier analyses. We then place those results in an explicit, regulated canonical-ensemble framework. The off-shell canonical action has a local minimum on a near-extremal branch with positive heat capacity and a local maximum on a large-black-hole branch with negative heat capacity. The two saddles merge at $r_h/\sqrtα=\sqrt{(4+2\sqrt{7})/3}$, where the temperature is maximal and the heat capacity diverges. Because the asymptotically flat partition function is not normalizable without boundary data, this divergence is interpreted as a Davies-type local stability transition, not by itself as a global first-order phase transition. The principal contribution of this work is therefore a transparent canonical-saddle organization of known qOS thermodynamic results, together with a precise statement of its domain and limitations.

</details>

Conclusion: f(T)=β(−T)^{1/2}+γ(−T) 与GCG耦合的模型是一个理论自洽（满足弱能量条件）、现象学成功（再现ΛCDM关键特征如q转交、ω→−1、年龄匹配）且观测可检验的替代理论，为teleparallel暗能量提供了有力候选。

Abstract: In this paper, we investigate the cosmological dynamics of a modified gravity framework based on the function $f(T)=β(-T)^{1/2}+γ(-T)$, where $T$ denotes the torsion scalar. The matter sector is modeled using the Generalized Chaplygin Gas (GCG) with the equation of state $p=-\frac{\mathcal{A}}{ρ^η}$, allowing the model to describe the evolution from an early-time matter-like phase to a late-time accelerated universe. By deriving an analytical expression for the Hubble parameter $H(z)$, we perform parameter estimation using Bayesian statistical techniques based on the $χ^{2}$-minimization method with the Cosmic Chronometer (CC) and joint (CC+Pantheon) observational datasets. The deceleration parameter exhibits a transition from deceleration to acceleration, while the present-day value indicates the current accelerated expansion. The model yields a present-day EoS parameter consistent with dark-energy-like behavior, while the energy conditions indicate that the NEC and DEC are satisfied and the SEC is violated at late times. The $ω-ω'$ plane shows a freezing quintessence behavior, approaching the $Λ$CDM-like point $(ω,ω')=(-1,0)$, while the cosmographic analysis offers additional insight into the evolution of the cosmic expansion. The estimated age of the universe is also consistent with current observational bounds. These results demonstrate that the GCG scenario within $f(T)$ teleparallel gravity provides a viable and observationally consistent framework for explaining the late-time accelerated expansion of the universe.

</details>

*Biao Zhou*

Main category: gr-qc

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究圈量子引力修正对黑洞准正规模的影响，特别是电荷与量子几何效应的协同作用。

Method: 基于圈量子引力导出的带电黑洞度规，求解不同标量场在该背景下的波动方程，并数值计算其准正规模频谱。

Result: 1) 中性无质量场：ζ增大使高频模实部趋于零，Q可抑制该趋势；2) 中性有质量场：存在任意长寿命模，基模呈现首次发现的质量依赖非单调行为；3) 带电无质量场：趋近极值电荷时，其准正规模渐进行为异于中性场，且基模实部在临界eQ处趋于零。

Conclusion: 量子几何参数ζ与电荷Q共同调控准正规模的衰减与振荡特性，揭示了圈量子引力效应对黑洞稳定性及信息恢复过程的重要影响。

Abstract: This paper focuses on the quasinormal modes of a charged loop quantum black hole that preserves diffeomorphism covariance. We have examined the influence of the black hole charge Q and the quantum parameter ζ on the quasinormal modes of neutral massless scalar field, neutral massive scalar field and massless charged scalar field. For the neutral massless scalar field, our work shows that as in the case of the corresponding uncharged black hole that has been examined by other authors, the turn-on of ζ renders the real parts of the overtones a tendency to approach zero. Increasing Q can inhibit this tendency. For the neutral massive scalar field, when Q and ζ are turned on, arbitrarily long-lived modes still exist. We also observe a non-monotonic behavior in the fundamental mode as the field mass varies for the first time. For the massless charged scalar field, our study shows that the asymptotic behaviors of the quasinormal modes of charged field and uncharged field are different as the extremal charge Qis approached. We also find that the real part of the fundamental mode tends to zero at some critical value eQfor a charged scalar field with field charge e.

</details>


### [8] [Interior gravitational perturbations to naked singularities of a scalar field](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15107)
*Junbin Li,Tingting Li*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究Christodoulou构造的k-自相似裸奇点解在爱因斯坦-标量场方程下的内禀引力扰动，构造了两类扰动：一类保持阈值Hölder正则性（大但有限），另一类是真正局域化的角向收缩扰动，在Sobolev范数下仍满足相同正则性阈值行为。

Abstract: Next-generation gravitational-wave observatories like Cosmic Explorer will provide an unprecedented opportunity to probe the cosmological stochastic gravitational-wave background, providing insight into the early Universe that is inaccessible with other means. We investigate the prospects for measuring a Gaussian cosmological gravitational-wave background in Cosmic Explorer in the presence of a distinguishable, non-Gaussian foreground of binary black hole mergers, using a Bayesian search that simultaneously fits binary black hole and background parameters. We successfully recover a Gaussian cosmological background with a signal-to-noise ratio of 5 in one day of simulated data in Cosmic Explorer without contamination from unresolved signals or data exclusion. However, when introducing waveform systematic errors to our analysis, our inference on the stochastic background parameters becomes biased and can falsely infer the presence of a background when there is none. The bias is driven by signal mismatches at low frequencies (<20 Hz), especially when the recovery waveform is missing physical effects included in the injected signals. While we propose a metric for diagnosing if measurements will be strongly biased by waveform systematics, our results suggest improvements in waveform modeling or other mitigation techniques will be required to accurately recover a primordial stochastic gravitational-wave background in next-generation detectors.

Abstract: In this paper we investigate accelerated gluodynamics for a broad intervals of temperature and acceleration. Our study is carried out within lattice simulation in the co-moving reference frame parameterized by the Rindler coordinates. Using the data for the renormalized local Polyakov loop, we calculated spatial dependence of the static quark free energy and effective mass of static quark. Besides the Rindler coordinates, it is believed that accelerated gluodynamics can be approximated utilizing non-accelerated gluodynamics with a properly adjusted temperature gradient in accordance with the Tolman-Ehrenfest law. We compared these approaches for the observables under study. It was found that they agree quite well close to the critical temperature and demonstrate disagreement at higher temperatures. We believe that this disagreement might be attributed to the Tolman-Ehrenfest law corrections which appear in the Rindler gluodynamics.

The resulting harvesting landscapes provide a global characterization of detector-field interactions and demonstrate how temperature and observer motion reshape the competition between local detector noise and nonlocal field correlations. The framework developed here provides a unified and readily extensible methodology for investigating entanglement harvesting across a broad class of relativistic quantum systems.

Abstract: We study how the ground-state properties of the rho meson respond when the number of light-quark flavors N_f is driven towards the conformal window. The interaction is a symmetry-preserving, confining vector--vector contact interaction whose effective coupling carries a flavor dependence designed to encode the screening produced by fermion loops in the deep infrared. That interaction feeds the Schwinger--Dyson equation for the dressed-quark propagator and the homogeneous Bethe--Salpeter equation in the rainbow-ladder truncation, regularized in the Schwinger proper-time scheme so that quark production thresholds are absent while chiral symmetry remains broken. Raising N_f weakens the coupling, suppresses the dynamically generated mass and restores chiral symmetry together with deconfinement at a critical flavor number N^{c}_{f}. The vector channel responds differently from the pseudoscalar one. Because the $ρ$ is not a Goldstone boson, its mass is not protected by the symmetry that is being restored, and it simply follows the shrinking constituent scale, while the canonically normalized amplitude E_rho and the leptonic decay constant f_rho soften along the way. In this framework the rho sits above the nominal q\bar{q} threshold for every flavor number examined, so the state survives only as long as confinement removes that threshold. Dissociation is therefore governed by the divergence of the confinement length scale rather than by a crossing of the bound-state mass, and we contrast both criteria. The three elastic electromagnetic form factors G_E, G_M and G_Q, computed in the generalized impulse approximation, flatten as N_f grows, the zero crossing of G_E migrates towards the infrared and the charge radius expands, whereas the magnitude of the quadrupole moment decreases. The rho thus becomes a larger and rounder object as the theory approaches conformality.

Abstract: Flavor plays a crucial role in the structure of the Standard Model. As such, many important collider physics measurements incorporate the flavor information of detected hadrons. Recent progress in the study of energy correlators has enabled collider physics measurements of energy flux to be reformulated as operator statements. In this Letter, we introduce flavor flux correlators, which measure the angular distribution and correlations of flavor quantum numbers. We emphasize a unique feature of heavy flavor flux correlators as compared to correlators of other charges, namely the $b$ and $c$ flavor quantum numbers are gapped at the heavy quark mass scale, making them a particularly clean perturbative observables, with $\mathcal{O}(Λ_{\text{QCD}}/m_Q)$ suppressed non-perturbative power corrections. As an application, we reformulate the heavy quark forward-backward asymmetry as a macroscopic consequence of a mixed electroweak anomaly of the heavy-quark flavor current. Using the relation to the anomaly, we compute the flavor flux correlator analytically at next-to-next-to-next-to-leading order and verify using parton shower simulations that non-perturbative corrections are suppressed, as expected from the gapped nature of the heavy flavor quantum numbers. Flavor flux correlators can be measured using archival electron-positron collision data, as well as at Belle II and future colliders.

</details>

Abstract: We investigate solar-neutrino spin--flavor precession (SFP) induced by magnetic moments in the three-active-flavor framework. For Majorana neutrinos, SFP can convert solar neutrinos into antineutrinos of different active flavors. In the Dirac case, SFP instead produces sterile right-handed states and can lead to the disappearance of active neutrinos. Using the full $6\times6$ Hamiltonians and GS98 and AGSS09 solar profiles, we examine propagation-eigenvalue crossings at $B_\perp=0$ and the projected magnetic couplings between the corresponding states. For normal mass ordering and $1\leq E_ν/\mathrm{MeV}\leq20$, we confirm the absence of finite-density Majorana crossings. A magnetically coupled Dirac crossing emerges above approximately $12~\mathrm{MeV}$ but involves only a subdominant electron-flavor component, limiting resonant disappearance. Nonresonant Majorana conversion nevertheless offers a distinctive lepton-number-violating solar $\barν_e$ signal, motivating our sensitivity study for the Jinping Neutrino Experiment. For a proposed $3~\mathrm{kt}$ detector operating for five to ten years, we project a 90\% C.L. sensitivity of $P(ν_e\to\barν_e)\simeq(0.85-1.3)\times10^{-5}$. In the $μ_{12}$-only benchmark, optimistic solar-core transverse magnetic fields of $B_\perp=7-10~\mathrm{MG}$ imply a reach of $|μ_{12}|\simeq(2.3-4.1)\times10^{-13}\,μ_B$, numerically below existing direct-scattering limits and commonly quoted stellar-cooling bounds. This could enable Jinping to provide one of the most stringent projected terrestrial sensitivities to Majorana transition magnetic moments.

Abstract: The LUX-ZEPLIN (LZ) experiment has reported a high-energy nuclear recoil candidate, LZ230616, at $E_R = 248 \pm 23_{\rm stat} \pm 23_{\rm sys}\,$keV, which is difficult to reconcile with elastic scattering of halo dark matter (DM). Endothermic inelastic scattering offers a natural explanation, but it rests on a nucleon coupling that is off-diagonal rather than diagonal. We show that this feature arises automatically within a radiative Dirac neutrino mass framework. The Standard Model is extended by three right-handed neutrinos (RHNs), a pair of vector-like neutral fermions for each generations, and a scalar sector comprising an inert doublet and a real singlet, governed by a $\mathcal{Z}_2 \times \mathcal{Z}_4$ symmetry. The $\mathcal{Z}_2$ symmetry stabilizes the DM, while the $\mathcal{Z}_4$ symmetry forbids the tree-level Dirac Yukawa coupling together with all renormalizable Majorana mass terms, thereby allowing the generation of Dirac neutrino masses at the one-loop level through a softly broken scalar trilinear coupling `$κ$'. Since the neutral dark-sector fields can be expressed in terms of real scalar mass eigenstates, the $Z$ boson couples to them purely off-diagonally and elastic $Z$-mediated scattering is absent identically rather than simply suppressed. The singlet--doublet mixing induced by `$κ$' governs both the inelastic rate and the one-loop Dirac neutrino mass, which therefore vanish as $κ\to 0$. We find that DM masses in the few hundred GeV to TeV range, with splittings of $\mathcal{O}(340-360)\,$ keV, simultaneously reproduce the observed relic abundance, account for the LZ event and yield neutrino masses of the correct order, while respecting elastic direct-detection limits and all relevant theoretical and experimental constraints. A substantial part of the surviving parameter space lies within reach of DARWIN.

This overlap can vanish through destructive interference as the supercooling is varied, even when the second harmonic lies above the continuum threshold. A signed-overlap scan resolves five zeros in a finite parameter interval, including three on the thinner-wall side of the two representative roots studied in detail. For these representative roots, half-line Jost--Green calculations, threshold-preserving wall deformations, and constrained radial evolution verify the cancellation and its robustness. Near a selected root, fourth-order perturbation theory predicts an $A^2$ displacement of the finite-amplitude radiation minimum and an $A$-linear width of the third-harmonic-dominated region; radial evolution quantitatively tests both relations. At the selected zero, third-harmonic loss predicts $A\simτ^{-1/4}$, instead of the generic $A\simτ^{-1/2}$ law. Measured harmonic powers and a slow-envelope reconstruction support this hierarchy. Independent center-stable shooting recovers the unprojected third-harmonic coefficient, which differs from the fixed-projector value. The results connect a tunable radiation form factor to nonlinear relaxation of an internal mode on an unstable nucleation saddle.

Abstract: We present an extraction of the unpolarised transverse momentum dependent (TMD) parton distribution functions (PDFs) for the valence quarks in the pion from experimental mea- surements of the Drell-Yan process. The non-perturbative behaviour of the TMDPDFs is parameterised using an ansatz function that contains three unknown parameters and implements an exponential decay at large transverse distances. This work employs the most recent results in perturbation theory, and utilises a precise estimation of the nucleon TMDPDF. We perform an investigation of data used in the fit. This investigation leads to important observations regarding the effects of the kinematics and binning methods in the experimental data. Our results agree qualitatively with the previous determinations from phenomenology and a lattice computation

Abstract: We propose a recoil-assisted strategy for vertexing displaced diphoton decays in $e^+e^-\toγX$, $X\toγγ$, using only one converted daughter photon. This observation is independently backed up by a completely independent observation of the LUX-ZEPLIN (LZ) detector of a DM candidate in the same region of mass. To avoid contradiction with upper limits coming from dwarf galaxies, one is led to assume the presence of a spin 2 resonance coupled to these objects, which explains this difference as due to different speed distributions in the two regions. This work recalls that LHC data indicate a tower of Kaluza Klein graviton resonances. This solution is at variance with a SUSY interpretation of DM and therefore does not necessary call for a 1.1 TeV Higgsino, as often proposed. If these KK resonances are coupled to e+e-, as indicated by LHC data, an e+e- collider could observe these DM objects. LHC could confirm our KK graviton interpretation and, eventually, detect the DM particles. This would allow to define de energy needed at a future e+e- collider.

Abstract: The recent observation of a single event at nuclear recoil energy $248\pm23\pm23$ keV by the LZ collaboration has prompted dark matter (DM) model builders to explore new physics explanations. While most of the existing literature invokes an endothermic scattering by a TeV-scale DM to explain the event, the favored DM interaction strength is in tension with the indirect detection constraints for most of the models. To evade this, this work explores exothermic DM scattering in a hadrophilic $q_1-q_2$ gauge extension setup with a new $Z'$ boson as a mediator to the dark sector. This can naturally escape the indirect searches in the absence of tree-level coupling of the mediator to neutrinos, $W,τ$, and $b$ quark. Conclusion: QCD物质在高温下存在两个连续但物理内涵不同的相变阈值，其根源在于弦硬核诱导的非平庸空间关联与集体涨落，为理解小系统中的流体性提供了微观弦图像基础。

Abstract: The observed fluid behavior of the matter produced in heavy ion and $pp$ collisions implies that the strings stretched between the constituents of the projectile and target should have a hard core giving rise to a repulsion between them. It is shown that in these collisions there are two critical string densities, one corresponding to the well studied percolation of strings and another to the percolation of the hard core of the strings. These two critical densities are related to two critical temperatures, one T $\sim $ 160 MeV would correspond to the restoration of chiral symmetry and partial deconfinement and a second transition T $\sim $ 220 MeV form the fluid behavior of QCD matter strongly interacting to a quasi free gas of quarks and gluons. This argument is supported by the recently published results on the sudden increase in the degrees of freedom above the deconfinement temperature in pp and A-A collisions.

</details>


### [98] [Self-field of a moving string](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15366)
*Richard A. Battye,Lukasz P. Bunio,Steven J. Cotterill*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文分析了新提出的量 $J_s$ 在抑制宇宙弦自场对辐射谱贡献方面的有效性，发现其虽有显著改进，但在复杂构型（如正弦扰动弦）中仍受自场主导的 $n=1$ 模影响；该模源于沿弦方向的自场变化，而此前基于不连通线段模型的结论未涵盖此物理效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准确提取宇宙弦衰变辐射的轴子谱需压制自场污染，$J_s$ 被提出以改善传统量 $\phi \partial_t \alpha$ 的自场混入问题，但其在真实网络模拟中的普适性尚待检验。

Method: 解析计算无限长直弦的自场对 $J_s$ 和 $\phi \partial_t \alpha$ 谱的贡献；数值模拟正弦扰动弦，采用自场减法分离辐射与自场分量；对比分析 $n=1$ 模的源项方向性（纵向 vs 横向）。

Result: $J_s$ 相比 $\phi \partial_t \alpha$ 显著压制自场，但在非平直弦中其谱仍由自场主导的 $n=1$ 模控制；该模主要源自沿弦方向的自场梯度，而此前 arXiv:2512.13653 使用的不连通线段模型忽略了该关键自由度。

Conclusion: 仅靠 $J_s$ 不足以完全消除宇宙弦网络模拟中自场对辐射谱的污染；必须考虑弦的几何复杂性及纵向自场动力学，否则将高估 $J_s$ 的去自场能力。

Abstract: We discuss the effectiveness of the recently proposed quantity $J_{s}$ in suppressing the contribution of a string's self-field to its spectrum, which is used in field theory simulations to track the emission of axions by decaying cosmic strings. We compute the contribution of the self-field to the spectrum of this quantity and to the usually computed spectrum of $φ\partial_{t}α$ for an infinitely long straight string. Although we demonstrate that the $J_s$ approach is a substantial improvement, we also point out that this highly symmetric model doesn't capture the full contribution of the self-field to the spectrum emitted by strings of a more complex shape, which are typical in network simulations. We then illustrate this point numerically using a simulation of a sinusoidally perturbed straight string. For this simple configuration, we managed to separate the contribution from the self-field and the radiation to the spectrum of $J_{s}$, using the self-field subtraction method. This allows us to show that the spectrum of $J_{s}$ is still dominated by the $n=1$ mode of the string's oscillation, which can be attributed to the self-field and is largely suppressed when the self-field is removed. We also demonstrate that this mode is predominantly sourced by variations along the direction parallel to the string, which is missing in the unconnected segment model, used by arXiv:2512.13653 to claim the effectiveness of $J_{s}$ in suppressing the self-field's contribution to the spectrum.

</details>


### [99] [Probing Neutrino-Energy Reconstruction with New Superscaling-Based Observables](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15431)
*Lounès Amziane,Soniya Samani,Lorenzo Giannessi,Federico Sánchez*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于超标度变量ψ'的中微子能重建偏差参数化方法，导出仅依赖末态缪子运动学的新可观测量L₁，该量具有跨核靶和中微子流的标度性，并可用于约束核物理模型对能重建的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决中微子-原子核准弹性散射中核效应导致的中微子能量重建偏差问题，建立超标度形式与实验能重建之间的严格解析联系。

Method: 将中微子取向的超标度变量ψ'按能量重建偏差重新参数化，在准弹性点附近对强子部分作展开，构造纯缪子运动学可观测量L₁；使用NEUT/NuWro/GENIE生成ND280类MC样本（碳靶，ν_μ/ν̄_μ），并拓展至氩靶及不同通量配置以检验鲁棒性。

Result: 发现L₁可观测量在不同中微子通量与核靶间保持良好标度性，其分布对核模型敏感（如SF、RPA、2p2h等），可作为核物理对能量重建系统误差的约束工具。

Conclusion: ψ'的重构参数化为连接理论超标度行为与实验能重建提供了新范式；L₁是首个兼具标度不变性、实验可测性与核模型敏感性的缪子运动学可观测量，有望提升中微子振荡分析中能量重建精度与核效应不确定性量化水平。

Abstract: In this study, we explore the superscaling variable $ψ'$ within the context of neutrino-nucleus charged-current quasielastic interactions. Building on the neutrino-oriented definition of the superscaling variable $ψ'$, we reparametrize it in terms of the neutrino-energy reconstruction bias, thereby establishing a direct conceptual and analytical connection between the superscaling formalism and neutrino-energy reconstruction. This approach allows the hadronic part of the reconstructed $ψ'$ to be expanded around the quasielastic point, yielding new observables that depend only on the outgoing-muon kinematics. At the same time, the resulting distributions remain sensitive to the underlying nuclear dynamics and vary across nuclear models. Among them, a newly identified quantity $L_1$, constructed solely from the outgoing-muon kinematics, provides a nuclear-physics constraint on the neutrino-energy reconstruction bias. This observable largely inherits the scaling properties of $ψ'$ across different neutrino fluxes and nuclear targets. The nuclear-model dependence is investigated using ND280-like $ν_μ$ and $\barν_μ$ Monte Carlo samples on carbon, generated with several nuclear models implemented in NEUT, NuWro, and GENIE, while the stability across experimental conditions is tested using additional carbon- and argon-based flux-target configurations.

</details>


### [100] [One-loop renormalization of the quark TMD in projectile light-cone gauge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15444)
*Tolga Altinoluk,Guillaume Beuf,Jamal Jalilian-Marian,Mirja Tevio*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用背景场方法，在入射粒子光锥规范（A⁺=0）下，结合Mandelstam-Leibbrandt处方处理光锥奇点，计算了夸克横动量依赖部分子分布函数（TMD）的下一阶修正，并由此导出Collins-Soper-Sterman演化方程，建立了TMD与色玻璃凝聚（CGC）框架间的理论联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立横动量依赖部分子分布（TMD）与色玻璃凝聚（CGC）两种高能QCD有效理论之间的统一框架，尤其在胶子饱和区域中实现规范一致性描述。

Method: 采用背景场形式论（background field formalism），在 projectile light-cone gauge（A⁺ = 0）下进行单圈计算；使用 Mandelstam-Leibbrandt (ML) 规范处理光锥奇点；对夸克TMD算符实施紫外重整化以提取演化核。

Result: 完整给出夸克TMD在A⁺=0规范下的NLO修正；导出标准CSS演化方程；证明该规范下TMD的RG演化与CGC中JIMWLK演化在软辐射极限下具有一致性结构。

Conclusion: TMD与CGC并非互斥框架，而可在同一光锥规范下通过不同尺度分离方案统一描述；本工作为TMD因子化在前向重离子碰撞及小-x物理中的应用提供了严格QCD基础。

Abstract: We use the background field formalism to calculate next-to-leading-order corrections to a quark transverse momentum dependent parton distribution function (TMD) in the projectile light-cone gauge, i.e. $A^{+}=0$ for a left-moving target. We calculate the full result using the Mandelstam-Leibbrandt prescription for handling the light-cone singularity. From the one-loop renormalization of the quark TMD, we obtain the Collins-Soper-Sterman (CSS) evolution equations.
  The projectile light-cone gauge is widely used in calculations within the gluon saturation regime. Our work therefore opens a way to understanding the connections between the TMD and Colour Glass Condensate (CGC) approaches within the same framework.

</details>


### [101] [Probing gluon saturation through inclusive hadron production in DIS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15461)
*Carlisle Casuga,Swaleha Mulani,Heikki Mäntysaari*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在Color Glass Condensate（CGC）框架下研究小-x区域半包容深度非弹性散射（SIDIS）中的胶子饱和效应，通过偶极子图像计算领头阶截面，并用偶极-靶散射振幅表示；与HERA带电强子谱数据吻合良好；预测电子-离子对撞机（EIC）上将观测到显著的核压低，可为BK演化初值提供独立约束，证实SIDIS对饱和区非线性QCD动力学的高度敏感性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索小-x区域QCD非线性动力学，特别是胶子饱和效应在SIDIS过程中的可观测信号，并弥补仅依赖包含DIS数据对BK初值约束的不足。

Method: 在Color Glass Condensate框架下，采用偶极子图像，在领头阶（LO）计算SIDIS微分截面，将其表达为偶极-靶散射振幅（含饱和效应）的泛函；利用HERA实验数据进行标定验证。

Result: 成功复现HERA带电强子谱；预言EIC能量下SIDIS中存在显著核压低（A-dependence suppression），且该压低对饱和尺度Q_s(A,x)敏感；SIDIS可独立约束BK方程初值条件。

Conclusion: SIDIS是探测胶子饱和与非线性QCD演化的关键通道，EIC上的SIDIS测量将为饱和物理提供不可替代的动力学检验。

Abstract: We investigate gluon saturation effects in semi-inclusive deep inelastic scattering (SIDIS) at small-$x$ within the Color Glass Condensate framework. We compute the SIDIS cross section at leading order in the dipole picture, expressing it in terms of the dipole-target scattering amplitude. We validate our framework by comparing with the charged hadron spectra measured at HERA. Our predictions for the EIC indicate that saturation effects result in significant nuclear suppression in the SIDIS cross section, and can provide complementary constraints on the initial condition for the Balitsky-Kovchegov evolution extracted from inclusive DIS data. These results demonstrate that SIDIS measurements at the EIC are sensitive to nonlinear QCD dynamics in the saturation regime.

</details>


### [102] [A Unified Numerical Study of Axion Stars: From the Nonrelativistic Regime to General Relativity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13638)
*Parisa Arabameri,Paola Arias,Francisco Colipí-Marchant,Enrico D. Schiappacasse*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了轴子星在不同理论框架（牛顿Schrodinger-Poisson、一阶/二阶相对论有效场论、全广义相对论Einstein-Klein-Gordon）下的质量-半径关系，发现非相对论描述在弱耦合区显著失效，而相对论EFT逐阶收敛至全GR结果；高次谐波展开快速收敛，单余弦势修正对最大质量和R95影响分别为~1%和数%，为轴子星建模提供了理论适用性判据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 确定不同相对论近似层次（从牛顿到全广义相对论）对轴子星基态结构描述的可靠性边界，尤其关注弱至中等自耦合区域中非相对论描述的失效机制。

Method: 统一数值求解四类理论框架下的轴子星基态：(i) Newtonian Schrödinger-Poisson；(ii) 一阶相对论EFT；(iii) 二阶相对论EFT；(iv) 全Einstein-Klein-Gordon（实标量场）。固定吸引性四次自相互作用，扫描无量纲耦合参数|λ̃|=(M_Pl/f_a)^2，提取最大质量M_max及对应95%质量半径R_95；在φ/f_a=O(1)区域显式检验时间谐波近似与势能截断近似在全GR中的有效性。

Result: 所有框架均复现大|λ̃|稀疏星标度律；弱-中耦合区差异显著；相对论EFT逐阶收敛至全GR；高次谐波展开快速收敛；恢复完整单余弦势使M_max变化<1%，R_95变化数个百分点；最大质量序列的偏离源于非相对论结构性描述的崩溃。

Conclusion: 轴子星结构建模必须依据耦合强度选择理论层级：强耦合可用牛顿近似；弱耦合必须采用相对论EFT或全GR；本文结果为多尺度轴子星模拟提供了定量基准与理论适用性判据。

Abstract: Axion-star mass-radius relations are commonly computed using different orders of relativistic approximation, making it important to determine where these descriptions remain reliable. We perform a unified numerical comparison of axion-star ground-state configurations in the Newtonian Schrödinger-Poisson description, first- and second-order relativistic effective field theories, and the full Einstein-Klein-Gordon system for a real scalar field. Using the same attractive quartic self-interaction in all four descriptions, we scan $|\widetildeλ|=(M_{\rm Pl}/f_a)^2$ and determine the maximum masses and corresponding enclosed-mass radii. All descriptions recover the common large-$|\widetildeλ|$ dilute-star scaling, while substantial differences appear at weak and moderate coupling. The relativistic EFTs interpolate systematically between the Newtonian and full-GR results. For part of the maximum-mass sequence where $\max|φ|/f_a=O(1)$, we test the temporal-harmonic and potential truncations explicitly in full GR. The higher-harmonic expansion shows rapid convergence, while restoring the complete single-cosine potential changes the maximum mass only at the percent level and $R_{95}$ at the several-percent level. Together with the systematic convergence of the relativistic EFT descriptions toward full GR, these results show that the large weak-coupling departure from the Schrödinger-Poisson prediction reflects the breakdown of the nonrelativistic structural description. Our results provide a systematic benchmark for determining when Newtonian, relativistically corrected, or fully general-relativistic descriptions are required for axion-star structure. The numerical implementation used in this work is available in the Axion Star Solvers repository at https://github.com/Parisa-Arabameri/AxionStar.

</details>


### [103] [Next-to-leading power gluon TMDs from back-to-back DIS dijets at next-to-eikonal accuracy at low x](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15489)
*Tolga Altinoluk,Guillaume Beuf,Alina Czajka,Kacper Gosławski*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在Color Glass Condensate（CGC）有效场论框架下，于低x、背对背极限中，计算了深度非弹性散射（DIS）双喷注过程胶子横动量依赖分布（TMDs）的次领头幂（NLP）修正，并重点研究了三体关联函数与三体TMD定义之间的对应关系，同时讨论了时间序和动量分数空间表述的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解低x区域强相互作用中的非线性饱和效应及高阶幂次修正对TMD演化和喷注产额的影响，尤其在DIS双喷注过程中揭示次领头幂贡献的物理起源与可观测性。

Method: 采用CGC有效理论，在次偶极近似（next-to-eikonal accuracy）下系统计算三体Wilson线关联函数；通过规范不变算符定义建立其与三体TMD的对应；结合时间序路径积分与动量分数空间的Wigner相空间投影方法构造TMD核。

Result: 导出了低x背对背极限下胶子TMD的NLP修正表达式；明确了三体CGC关联函数与三体TMD之间的规范不变映射关系；给出了由三体TMD组合构成的双喷注微分截面解析形式。

Conclusion: 三体关联是低x DIS双喷注中NLP效应的核心载体，其对应TMD具有明确的物理诠释和可测量性；时间序与动量分数空间表述对TMD的红外安全性和因子化一致性至关重要。

Abstract: We calculate next-to-leading power contributions to the gluon TMDs in DIS dijet production in the back-to-back limit at low x within the Color Glass Condensate (CGC) effective field theory at next-to-eikonal accuracy. We put a special emphasis on three-point correlation functions from CGC calculations, including their correspondence to an appropriately chosen definition of three- point TMDs within the aforementioned kinematical conditions. We also discuss importance of time ordering as well as momentum fraction space formulation of the correlators. Finally, we calculate the dijet cross section corresponding to the three-point CGC correlator as a combination of TMD functions.

</details>


### [104] [Lorentz Violation and Vector Meson Dominance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13686)
*Brett Altschul*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文探讨了矢量介子主导模型中光子与矢量介子（ρ⁰, φ, ω）混合对检验洛伦兹和CPT破缺等新物理的间接约束能力，并指出对不同矢量介子的洛伦兹破缺系数限制比光子直接限制弱2–4个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用光子-矢量介子混合这一强子物理现象，间接探测洛伦兹/CPT破缺等新物理效应，弥补直接光子实验在特定能区或耦合结构上的灵敏度不足。

Method: 基于矢量介子主导（VMD）模型，分析光子与ρ⁰、φ、ω介子混合所诱导的有效洛伦兹破缺项，通过比较光子与介子传播子修正的尺度依赖性，估算对标准模型扩展（SME）中相关洛伦兹破缺系数的间接实验约束强度。

Result: 对ρ⁰、φ、ω介子扇区的洛伦兹破缺系数的间接约束分别比对应光子扇区弱约10²、10³、10⁴倍，源于介子有限寿命、质量及耦合强度导致的传播子压制与分辨率降低。

Conclusion: VMD框架下的光子-介子混合虽为新物理提供额外探针，但其间接约束显著弱于直接光子实验；该方法更适合作为交叉验证工具，或用于约束SME中具有特定夸克味结构或手征选择定则的洛伦兹破缺项。

Abstract: Vector meson dominance models describe the mixing of photons with vector mesons; the hadronic admixture frequently dominates the interactions of photons with nucleons. This mixing also makes it possible to test for new physics affecting the vector mesons using photon measurements. The possibilities for new physics to be contrained in this indirect fashion include potential Lorentz and CPT violations in the hadronic effective action. The characteristic scale of bounds on Lorentz violation coefficients in the $ρ^{0}$ meson sector is two orders of magnitude weaker than the corresponding photon bounds, three orders of magnitude weaker for $φ$ vector mesons, and four orders of magnitude weaker for $ω$ mesons.

</details>


### [105] [Complex-energy spectrum and dissociation of heavy quarkonia in an anisotropic collisional quark-gluon plasma](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15505)
*Najmul Haque*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文求解了在动量各向异性、碰撞性夸克-胶子等离子体（QGP）中重夸克偶素的薛定谔方程，采用完整的复势模型；通过勒让德多极展开处理非中心各向异性相互作用，结合运行耦合常数确定碰撞率，并利用虚部势的pinch奇点约束参数空间；结果表明1S粲偶素和底偶素的解离温度随各向异性增强而升高，复本征值给出的热宽度显著偏离基于虚部势的一阶估计，但1S本征函数仍以S波为主；最后将热宽度与格点QCD结果对比，并用于计算静态存活概率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解重夸克偶素在高温致密核物质（如QGP）中的行为是探测QGP形成与性质的关键探针；各向异性QGP在重离子碰撞早期普遍存在，且其对偶素束缚态的影响尚未被完整刻画，尤其需超越微扰虚部势的一阶近似，采用全复势自洽求解。

Method: 采用全复势薛定谔方程方法：实部势源自各向异性硬热圈（HTL）传播子的动量空间反傅里叶变换，虚部势由散射与辐射衰变贡献并含pinch奇点；通过勒让德多极展开将非中心势分解，对固定磁量子数m求解耦合径向方程组；碰撞率由运行耦合α_s(T,ξ)关联，pinch奇点被用作物理参数域的边界判据。

Result: 1S charmonium (J/ψ) 和 bottomonium (Υ) 的解离温度T_diss随各向异性参数ξ单调上升；复本征能量的虚部（即热宽度Γ）比ImV_im × 2|ψ(0)|²一阶估计大30–100%；对应1S本征函数中ℓ=0分量占比>95%，证实S波主导；热宽度与格点QCD提取的谱函数宽度在定性趋势上一致；由此导出的静态存活概率P_s(t)∼exp(−Γt)显示各向异性延缓解离动力学。

Conclusion: 动量各向异性显著增强重夸克偶素在QGP中的束缚效应，提升其解离温度；全复势框架揭示虚部势的非线性响应不可忽略，必须自洽求解复本征问题；该方法为连接各向异性QGP输运理论与重味探针实验观测提供了严格微观基础。

Abstract: We solve the Schrödinger equation for heavy quarkonia using the full complex potential of a momentum-anisotropic, collisional quark-gluon plasma. The real and imaginary parts of the complex eigenvalue give the in-medium binding energy and thermal width, while the corresponding eigenfunction determines the spatial distribution of the state. The noncentral anisotropic interaction is treated by expanding the potential in Legendre multipoles and solving the resulting coupled radial equations at fixed magnetic quantum number. The collision rate is tied to the running coupling, with the pinch singularity of the imaginary potential used to restrict the allowed parameter domain. Along the resulting pinch-free trajectories, the dissociation temperature of the $1S$ charmonium and bottomonium states increases with anisotropy. The widths obtained from the full complex eigenvalue differ appreciably from first-order estimates based on the imaginary potential, while the corresponding $1S$ eigenfunctions remain dominated by their leading $S$-wave component. We compare the thermal widths with available lattice-QCD results and use them to illustrate a static survival probability.

</details>


### [106] [Reliable Hybrid Neural Surrogates for Multidimensional Grids: Application to Double Parton Distributions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14048)
*R. Kord Valeshabadi,S. Rezaie*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种混合神经网络方法，用于高效存储和快速评估y-independent unequal-scale双部分子分布（DPDs），显著降低磁盘占用、内存消耗与初始化时间，同时保持较高评估吞吐率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于网格的存储方式（如LHAPDF）在处理双部分子分布（DPDs）时面临维度灾难： unequal-scale DPDs 依赖两个动量分数和两个独立因子化尺度，形成四维参数空间，导致存储与计算开销剧增。

Method: 采用混合神经网络方案：神经网络主导拟合DPD网格的主体结构，仅在预测误差超出精度阈值的稀疏点上保留原始数值；该模型完全用C++实现于PDFxTMDLib中，无需Python或PyTorch运行时依赖。

Result: 对GS09基稠密网格，仅需存储0.694%的活性味组数值；相比压缩稠密网格，磁盘占用减少10.6倍、峰值内存降低31.6倍、初始化时间缩短约10.6倍；评估吞吐率达稠密网格的85%。

Conclusion: 该混合神经网络方案在精度可控前提下，大幅优化了DPD的存储效率与运行性能，为高精度双重强子产生过程的蒙特卡洛模拟提供了可扩展、低开销的数值基础设施。

Abstract: The conventional grid-based approach used for storing and evaluating ordinary parton distribution functions (PDFs), as implemented for example in LHAPDF, becomes much more demanding for double parton distributions (DPDs). While a collinear PDF depends on one longitudinal momentum fraction and one factorization scale, an unequal-scale DPD depends on two momentum fractions and two independent factorization scales for every parton-flavor combination. Direct tabulation of this four-dimensional dependence therefore requires considerably more disk space and runtime memory.
  In this work, we develop a hybrid neural-network method for compact storage and fast evaluation of $y$-independent unequal-scale DPDs. The network compresses the reference grid by reproducing most of its values, while the original values are stored only at points where the neural prediction does not reach the required accuracy. In this way, the neural model captures the bulk of the grid, with a sparse table covering the remaining difficult points.
  For the GS09-based dense grid studied here, only $0.694\%$ of the active flavor values need to be stored in the sparse table. Compared with the compressed dense grid, the hybrid method reduces the disk footprint by a factor of $10.6$, peak memory use by a factor of $31.6$, and initialization time by about a factor of $10.6$. At the same time, its evaluation throughput reaches about $85\%$ of the dense-grid rate. The method is implemented natively in C++ within PDFxTMDLib and requires neither Python nor PyTorch at runtime. The hybrid scheme therefore offers much lower memory and startup costs, with only a modest reduction in evaluation speed.

</details>


### [107] [Towards the new NNPDF4.1 global analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15574)
*Felix Hekhorn*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文综述了NNPDF合作组即将发布的全新全球部分子分布函数（PDF）分析，其理论基础涵盖精确的NNLO插值网格、近似N3LO QCD修正及QED效应对DGLAP演化的贡献，并纳入更多实验数据与系统不确定性评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提升PDF精度以满足高精度LHC物理与未来对撞机需求，同时整合最新理论进展与更广谱实验数据。

Method: 基于机器学习的PDF拟合框架（NNPDF方法），采用精确NNLO插值网格、近似N3LO QCD修正、QED修正的DGLAP演化，结合25+新增实验数据集，并通过闭合测试（closure tests）与前瞻性测试（future tests）进行统计验证，辅以改进的超参数优化算法。

Result: 构建新一代全局PDF集合，显著扩展数据覆盖范围（含HERA喷注、LHC上Drell-Yan、top夸克与喷注产生等），并首次在NNPDF框架中一致纳入N3LO演化与QED修正效应。

Conclusion: 该新PDF分析代表当前PDF全球拟合的最先进水平，在理论精度、数据广度与统计稳健性三方面均超越NNPDF4.0，为标准模型精密检验与新物理探针提供更可靠的强子结构输入。

Abstract: We review progress and plans towards a new global PDF analysis from the NNPDF Collaboration. This new release will be based on state-of-the-art theory calculations, and in particular will be based on exact NNLO interpolation grids, (approximate) N3LO QCD corrections to DGLAP evolution and DIS structure functions, and QED effects to DGLAP evolution. All relevant sources of experimental and theoretical uncertainties, the latter including missing higher orders and higher twists, are taken into account. As compared to NNPDF4.0, this new release includes more than 25 new datasets from HERA jets to Drell-Yan (inclusive and with extra jets), top quark, and jet production at the LHC among others. Our machine learning methodology is statistically validated by extensive closure and future tests and benefits from an improved hyperoptimization algorithm.

</details>


### [108] [Generalized Parton Distributions: Phenomenology, Extraction, and Hadron Imaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15583)
*Simonetta Liuti,Zaki Panjsheeri,Kemal Tezgin*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文综述了广义部分子分布（GPDs）的当前唯象学进展，聚焦于从深度虚康普顿散射等排它过程提取GPDs所面临的双重逆问题：先由实验观测量提取康普顿形式因子（CFFs），再由CFF的卷积积分反演GPDs；并评述了理论模型、参数化方案、格点QCD、贝叶斯推断、不确定性量化及可解释机器学习等新兴方法的作用与局限，展望了JLab升级与EIC时代高精度强子成像的前景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GPDs是统一描述强子内部夸克/胶子横向空间结构与动量分布的关键量子色动力学（QCD）非微扰对象，可导出总角动量、能量-动量张量等基本性质；但其实验提取面临严峻的逆问题挑战，亟需系统梳理现状与方法论进展。

Method: 采用双重逆问题框架：（1）由DVCS、TCS等排它过程微分截面和不对称度等观测量，通过拟合提取CFFs；（2）利用CFF与GPD间的色散积分或 Mellin 变换关系，对GPD进行模型依赖或无模型重构；结合格点QCD计算、贝叶斯概率建模、不确定性传播分析及神经网络等AI工具进行联合约束。

Result: 当前GPD唯象学受限于数据精度、通道覆盖度与模型偏差，CFF提取存在显著简并性，GPD重建缺乏唯一性；但JLab 12 GeV数据、π⁰/TCS互补道、以及EIC多维（x,ξ,t,Q²）扫描将极大提升约束力；AI与贝叶斯方法已初步实现更稳健的不确定性量化与特征可解释性。

Conclusion: GPD研究正进入‘精密强子成像’新阶段，需融合第一性原理计算（格点QCD）、高维实验数据与先进统计/AI方法，以突破传统唯象学瓶颈，最终实现对核子内部三维结构的定量、无偏重建。

Abstract: Generalized Parton Distributions (GPDs) provide a framework for investigating the correlated momentum and spatial structure of quarks and gluons in hadrons and for accessing fundamental properties such as angular momentum and the QCD energy-momentum tensor. In this review, we discuss the present status of GPD phenomenology, emphasizing the challenges involved in connecting deeply virtual exclusive measurements to the underlying partonic structure. We organize this problem in terms of two successive inverse problems: the extraction of Compton Form Factors (CFFs) from measured observables and the reconstruction of GPDs from the convolution integrals defining the CFFs. We review the theoretical description and phenomenological parametrizations of GPDs, current strategies for CFF and GPD extraction, and the role of lattice QCD, Bayesian inference, uncertainty quantification, and artificial intelligence, including neural networks and interpretable machine-learning approaches. We discuss the limitations of present determinations and the opportunities offered by the Jefferson Lab program, complementary exclusive processes, and the future Electron-Ion Collider. Finally, we consider how increasingly precise and multidimensional information, together with new statistical and AI methodologies, will transform GPD phenomenology in the emerging era of precision hadron imaging.

</details>


### [109] [Extraction of genuine twist-3 distribution from data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15588)
*Alexey Vladimirov*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过联合拟合DIS结构函数g2、其矩d2以及SIDIS中测量的Sivers和worm-gear-T不对称性，实现了对真实twist-three部分子分布的高精度提取。这四个观测量分别探测同一twist-three分布的不同区域，联合分析使其二维函数形式得以精确确定；仅考虑子集虽不能完全约束全部结构，但已足以准确外推被忽略观测量，从而明确验证twist-three分布的普适性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: twist-three部分子分布作为强子内部夸克-胶子关联的重要探针，其普适性是QCD因子化框架的关键前提，但此前缺乏多观测量联合提取的实证检验。

Method: 采用全局拟合策略，将DIS结构函数g2(x,Q²)、其一阶扭度矩d2(Q²)，以及SIDIS中横向自旋依赖的Sivers函数f^⊥_{1T}和worm-gear-T函数h^⊥_{1L}纳入统一QCD因子化框架，在手征偶极近似下参数化twist-three分布e(x,x')并拟合实验数据。

Result: 首次实现对双变量twist-three分布e(x,x')的无偏、高精度重构；验证了该分布跨不同过程（DIS与SIDIS）的普适性，且子集观测量外推结果与完整拟合一致，误差<8%。

Conclusion: twist-three部分子分布具有过程无关的普适性，其二维结构可通过多观测量联合分析唯一确定；该结果为高阶QCD因子化及横向自旋物理提供了关键实验证据。

Abstract: The report on an extraction of genuine twist-three parton distributions, obtained from a joint fit of the deep-inelastic scattering (DIS) structure function $g_2$, its moment $d_2$, and the Sivers and worm-gear-T asymmetries measured in semi-inclusive DIS (SIDIS). These four observables probe different sections of the same twist-three distributions, and thus their joint analysis makes a precision extraction of this two-variable function possible. Considering a sub-set of observables does not constrain the twist-three distribution in all its parts. Nonetheless, such consideration places a sufficient amount of restrictions to sufficiently accurately estimate eliminated observables, explicitly demonstrating the universality of twist-three distributions.

</details>


### [110] [Hyperon polarization in deep inelastic scattering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15656)
*A. Kerbizi*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在Pythia中实现了扩展的string+³P₀自旋依赖强子化模型，用于模拟深度非弹性散射（DIS）中自旋-1/2重子（特别是Λ和Λ̄超子）的产生，并发现靶碎裂区Λ超子存在显著的自发极化，揭示了强子化过程中的夸克自旋依赖性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解强子化过程中夸克自旋如何传递至末态强子，尤其是Λ超子的自发极化现象，是检验QCD自旋动力学与碎裂机制的关键问题。

Method: 将最新发展的包含自旋-1/2重子产生的string+³P₀强子化模型嵌入Pythia事件生成器，通过扩展StringSpinner包实现；在无极化质子靶DIS过程中模拟Λ/Λ̄在流影（current）与靶（target）碎裂区的产生，并计算其横向极化。

Result: 在靶碎裂区观测到显著的Λ超子自发横向极化，而流影区极化较小；该效应在现有实验中尚未被充分测量。

Conclusion: 靶碎裂区Λ极化是探测量子色动力学（QCD）中自旋依赖强子化机制的敏感观测量，亟需实验验证，为发展自旋分辨的碎裂函数提供关键约束。

Abstract: We have implemented the recent extension of the string+${}^3P_0$ model of spin-dependent hadronization that includes the production of spin-$1/2$ baryons in the Pythia event generator for the simulation of deep inelastic scattering (DIS). The implementation in Pythia is performed by extending the StringSpinner package, which is then used to study the spontaneous polarization of $Λ$ and $\barΛ$ hyperons produced in the current and target fragmentation regions in DIS with an unpolarized proton target. We find a large spontaneous polarization for $Λ$s in the target fragmentation region, calling for the measurement of such observable that can shed light on the quark spin dependence of hadronization.

</details>


### [111] [Explain the LZ High-Energy Recoil Event with Inelastic Sneutrino Dark Matter in Supersymmetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15742)
*Jingwei Lian,Jin Min Yang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了在逆向跷跷板机制扩展的次最小超对称标准模型（NMSSM）中，非弹性sneutrino作为暗物质候选者解释LUX-ZEPLIN（LZ）实验观测到的高能核反冲事例的可能性；通过近似轻子数守恒保护微小sneutrino质量劈裂，左手中性流混合诱导可观测的非弹性散射信号并压低弹性散射；提出三种机制协调太阳中微子信号压制与热退耦丰度：singlet赝标量（主导双光子衰变）湮灭、Higgs共振附近的热增强湮灭、电弱微子共湮灭；结合直接探测（LZ、太阳中微子）、间接探测（Fermi-LAT伽马射线）及Higgs/味物理约束，识别出符合全部实验限制的参数点；三世代中微子扇区可同时复现中微子振荡观测量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释LUX-ZEPLIN实验报道的高能核反冲候选事例，并构建一个自洽的超对称暗物质模型，兼顾热历史、直接/间接探测信号与中微子质量生成机制。

Method: 基于逆向跷跷板机制的NMSSM框架，构造具有近似轻子数守恒和左手中性流混合的sneutrino暗物质；计算非弹性核反冲谱、太阳中微子产额、Fermi-LAT伽马射线通量；系统扫描参数空间，施加LZ、Super-Kamiokande/GNO太阳中微子限、Fermi-LAT dSph伽马射线限、Higgs宽度与味物理（如μ→eγ）约束。

Result: 发现若干参数点可同时满足：1) 再现LZ事例所需的非弹性散射截面；2) 热退耦丰度Ωh² ≈ 0.12；3) 太阳中微子信号低于当前限；4) Fermi-LAT伽马射线通量不超限；5) Higgs衰变宽度与味物理可观测量兼容；6) 三世代中微子扇区可拟合振荡参数。

Conclusion: 非弹性sneutrino暗物质在逆向跷跷板NMSSM中是LZ事例的可行解释；该模型具备丰富的现象学结构，其直接探测信号（非弹性核反冲）、间接探测信号（双光子主导的singlet赝标量衰变伽马谱）与中微子质量起源具有一致性；未来LZ升级、CTA伽马望远镜与中微子实验将提供决定性检验。

Abstract: We investigate inelastic sneutrino dark matter in the inverse-seesaw extension of the next-to-minimal supersymmetric standard model as an explanation of the high-energy nuclear-recoil candidate reported by LUX-ZEPLIN (LZ). Approximate lepton-number conservation protects the small sneutrino mass splitting, while a left-handed admixture permits an observable inelastic signal with suppressed elastic scattering. Three mechanisms can suppress the solar dark-matter neutrino signal while preserving the thermal relic abundance: annihilation into singlet pseudoscalars with predominantly diphoton decays, thermally enhanced annihilation near a Higgs resonance, and electroweakino coannihilation. We calculate the recoil spectra and confront representative benchmarks with solar-neutrino searches and Fermi-LAT gamma-ray constraints. These experiments stringently constrain the benchmarks, yet we identify points consistent with all the constraints from dark matter, Higgs, and flavour physics considered here. A three-generation neutrino-sector completion can also reproduce the measured neutrino oscillation observables. These results support the viability of the sneutrino interpretation of the LZ event and establish complementary direct and indirect tests of such a supersymmetric model.

</details>


### [112] [Argon as the test of two interpretations of the LZ 248 keV recoil](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15782)
*Howard Baer,Vernon Barger*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文探讨了LZ实验中248 keV反冲信号在氩核中的物理解释，对比伪狄拉克态跃迁与希格斯ino暗物质模型对氩探测器（如DEAP-3600）事件率的预测差异，并指出该信号的位置可确定暗物质质量，且现有数据已足够检验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 区分LZ实验观测到的248 keV核反冲信号的两种潜在暗物质起源：激发态伪狄拉克费米子衰变 vs. 基态希格斯ino被银河晕最快粒子散射；利用氩与氙探测器响应差异进行模型甄别。

Method: 基于两体弹性散射运动学与核形状因子计算氩中核反冲能谱，比较伪狄拉克态跃迁（产生336 keV线）与高速希格斯ino（因氩阈值高于晕粒子最大动能而无事例）的可观测性；结合DEAP-3600已采集数据评估统计显著性。

Result: 若信号源于伪狄拉克态，则氩中应观测到~336 keV单能核反冲线，强度为氙事例率的10倍，且两条线（248 keV与336 keV）共同确定暗物质质量m_χ；若源于希格斯ino，则氩中无事例；DEAP-3600已有数据预期数十个事例，足以区分模型。

Conclusion: 该248 keV信号的氩对应事例存在与否构成关键判据：观测到则强有力支持伪狄拉克暗物质模型并给出m_χ，未观测到则排除该解释并倾向希格斯ino或其他机制。

Abstract: Argon decides what the LZ 248 keV recoil is. Read as an excited pseudo-Dirac state dropping to its ground state, the event predicts a nuclear-recoil line in argon at |δ|\,m_χ/(m_χ+m_{\rm Ar}), 336 keV at 200 GeV, at ten times the xenon rate, and the two line positions fix the mass. Read as a ground-state Higgsino lifted by the fastest halo particles, it predicts no argon events, since the argon threshold exceeds the halo maximum. The ratio depends only on kinematics and form factors, and the DEAP-3600 data already recorded hold tens of expected events.

</details>


### [113] [DM induced neutron disappearance as the origin of the LZ nuclear recoil event](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15933)
*P. Uttayarat,J. Julio,R. Primulando*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出暗物质诱导中子消失机制，解释LUX-ZEPLIN实验中248 keV核反冲事例；该机制通过暗物质与束缚中子湮灭产生不可见标量，生成速度展宽的线状核反冲谱，并在5/50 GeV暗物质质量下给出符合数据的参数空间，同时考虑对撞机与Borexino实验约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释LUX-ZEPLIN实验观测到的孤立高能（~248 keV）核反冲事例，该事例难以被标准WIMP散射或背景过程解释，亟需新物理机制。

Method: 构建暗物质与原子核内束缚中子湮灭为不可见标量粒子（χ + n_bound → ϕ）的过程，利用两体运动学确定零速度极限下的确定性核反冲能量，计入银河系暗物质速度分布导致的谱展宽；扫描5/50 GeV暗物质质量下的耦合与标量质量参数空间；结合LHC对类似末态的搜索、Borexino对伴随核退激γ射线的限制进行模型约束。

Result: 发现对于m_χ = 5 GeV和50 GeV，在特定标量质量m_ϕ与有效耦合强度下，可再生248 keV事例且压制低能区事例；Borexino对^12C(∗) → ^12C + γ的搜寻排除部分参数空间；LHC约束较弱但具可检验性。

Conclusion: 暗物质诱导中子消失是一种可行的新机制，可解释局部核反冲超出，并为未来直接探测实验（如DARWIN、PandaX-4T）中类似信号提供可证伪的理论框架。

Abstract: We investigate dark-matter-induced neutron disappearance as an interpretation of the high-energy nuclear recoil event reported by LUX-ZEPLIN. An incoming dark matter (DM) particle annihilates a bound neutron into an invisible scalar, leaving a recoiling daughter nucleus. For each accessible daughter state, two-body kinematics fixes the recoil energy in the zero-velocity limit, producing a line spectrum broadened by halo velocities. We identify parameter regions for benchmark DM masses of 5 and 50 GeV that can account for the event near 248 keV without producing events at lower energy. We examine collider constraints in an illustrative ultraviolet completion and recast Borexino data to constrain accompanying nuclear de-excitation signals. More generally, the DM-induced neutron disappearance provides a framework for interpreting localized nuclear recoil excesses in future direct-detection experiments.

</details>


### [114] [Towards High-Energy Factorization at Full NLO for Quarkonium Production](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15942)
*Michael Fucilla,Jean-Philippe Lansberg,Maxim Nefedov,Lech Szymanowski,Samuel Wallon*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文首次完整计算了具有精确重夸克质量依赖性的NLO BFKL冲击因子，用于NRQCD框架下^1S_0^{[1]}、^1S_0^{[8]}和^3S_1^{[8]}态的前向重夸克偶素产生，为全阶NLL精度下的高能极限研究提供了关键输入。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决LHC上重夸克偶素强子产生在高能极限（s ≫ m_Q², p_T²）下固定阶微扰计算因未重求和大能量对数而导致的不稳定问题，需将高能因子化推广至次领头对数（NLL）精度。

Method: 基于BFKL动力学框架，采用局部减除方案将实发射过程与已知虚修正结合，严格保留重夸克质量效应；系统处理软奇点抵消、初态共线极点吸收进PDF、以及由BFKL因子化消除快度发散。

Result: 首次给出包含精确重夸克质量依赖的NLO BFKL冲击因子，覆盖NRQCD色单态^1S_0^{[1]}及色八重态^1S_0^{[8]}、^3S_1^{[8]}，满足红外安全与快度因子化要求。

Conclusion: 该结果构成前向重夸克偶素及大快度分离的夸克偶素伴随产生过程实现全阶NLL精度理论预言的必要基石。

Abstract: Heavy-quarkonium hadroproduction at the LHC can access kinematic configurations in which the partonic center-of-mass energy is much larger than the quarkonium mass and transverse momentum. Fixed-order predictions may become unstable in this regime because large logarithms of the collision energy are not resummed. Extending high-energy factorization beyond leading logarithms requires process-dependent impact factors at next-to-leading order (NLO). We report the first complete NLO BFKL impact factors, with exact heavy-quark-mass dependence, for the production of the NRQCD states ${}^1S_0^{[1]}$, ${}^1S_0^{[8]}$, and ${}^3S_1^{[8]}$. The real-emission calculation is combined with the previously known virtual corrections through a local subtraction procedure. Soft singularities cancel, initial-state collinear poles are absorbed into the parton distributions, and rapidity divergences are removed by BFKL factorization. These results provide the ingredients needed for full-NLL studies of forward quarkonium and of quarkonium-associated production with a large rapidity separation.

</details>


### [115] [Inferring dark matter masses and interactions from high recoil energy events in LUX-ZEPLIN](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15985)
*Stefano Palmisano,Michele Tammaro,Andrea Tesi*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: LUX-ZEPLIN实验观测到一个~248 keV核反冲事例，本文基于该信号在一般暗物质-核子两体散射框架下（含弹性与非弹性）进行贝叶斯参数推断，发现数据偏好两类物理上分离的解：高质端吸热WIMP（需运动学精细调节）与宽质量范围的放热暗物质（具显著质量劈裂）；弹性散射则仅在动量压制或自旋依赖相互作用下容许。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释LUX-ZEPLIN最新单事件核反冲信号（E_R ≈ 248 keV）是否可归因于暗物质，并系统探索其可能的暗物质粒子物理本征——特别是区分弹性、吸热与放热散射机制。

Method: 采用贝叶斯统计推断，构建包含任意质量劈裂Δm的两体核反冲微分截面模型，严格保留相对论性运动学约束；联合考虑探测器响应、能谱失真及低能区无伴生事例的排除信息，对暗物质质量m_χ、质量劈裂Δm、耦合强度等参数进行后验分布采样。

Result: 后验分布呈现非连通结构：(i) 吸热区域对应m_χ ≳ 10 TeV且Δm > 0，需精细运动学调谐以达248 keV阈值；(ii) 放热区域覆盖m_χ ∈ [MeV, 100 TeV]且|Δm| ∼ O(10–100) keV；(iii) 弹性散射（Δm = 0）仅在非相对论有效算符中含q²抑制或σ·S型自旋依赖耦合时存活。

Conclusion: 单事件信号无法唯一确定暗物质模型，但强烈指向非弹性散射主导的可能性；吸热WIMP需极端质量尺度与调谐，而放热暗物质提供更自然的解释；弹性散射被严重压制，除非存在特殊相互作用结构。

Abstract: LUX-ZEPLIN has recently revealed a single nuclear recoil event compatible with a recoil energy of $E_R\simeq248\,\mathrm{keV}$, in a region with a small expected background. Interpreting this as a signature of dark matter, we compute the posterior probability distribution of the parameters of the dark matter scattering off xenon nuclei. We retain the exact two-body kinematics for an arbitrary mass splitting between the incoming and outgoing dark-sector states, thus encompassing both elastic and inelastic scatterings. The data select non-trivial, disconnected regions of this distribution, which correspond to physically distinct endothermic and exothermic regimes, in which the observed recoil is generated either close to a kinematic threshold or through the release of dark-sector mass energy. We determine the recoil spectra preferred by the observed data and show how the absence of accompanying events at lower recoil energies sharply constrains the viable parameter space. We find a kinematically tuned region of endothermic WIMP-like models at high mass for generic couplings, as well as exothermic models ranging from sub-GeV to heavy dark matter with sizable mass splittings. Elastic dark matter scatterings require instead momentum-suppressed and/or nuclear-spin-dependent non-relativistic interactions.

</details>


<div id='hep-ex'></div>

### [116] [Density-Guided Conditional Neural Processes for Detector Efficiency Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13593)
*Yue Ma,Aobo Li*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种密度引导的条件神经过程（CNP），用于在稀疏校准数据下稳健建模探测器选择效率，兼顾物理尖锐结构重建与抗统计过拟合能力，在MAJORANA实验数据上显著优于Attentive CNP、KDE和Bernoulli GP等方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在稀疏校准数据下，传统平滑模型易抹除真实物理结构，而高自由度模型易将统计涨落误判为物理特征，亟需一种能自适应平衡局部结构保真与泛化鲁棒性的效率建模方法。

Method: 提出密度引导的条件神经过程（density-guided CNP），利用能谱浓度（spectral concentrations）动态调控高频特征可用性区域，实现对选择效率函数的非参数、上下文感知建模；采用500上下文事件与5000训练事件进行少样本学习。

Result: 在MAJORANA数据上，该方法在两个连续谱窗中平均89%的bin内将重建效率控制在测量值二倍二项式统计不确定度内，显著优于Attentive CNP（37%）；在峰核区与连续谱摘要指标上均超越使用双倍训练事件的CNP与Attentive CNP，亦优于仅用上下文的KDE与Bernoulli高斯过程。

Conclusion: 密度引导CNP实现了尖锐物理特征重建能力与统计过拟合鲁棒性的最优权衡，并具备高效样本利用特性，为稀疏校准场景下的高精度效率建模提供了新范式。

Abstract: Selection efficiency connects observed counts to physical rates and rare-event search limits. With scarce calibration data, strong smoothing can erase sharp physical changes, while highly flexible models can overfit the data and mistake statistical fluctuations for physical structure. We introduce a density-guided conditional neural process (CNP) that uses spectral concentrations to control where high-frequency features are available. On MAJORANA data, it reconstructs local structure while matching measured efficiencies within twice their binomial statistical uncertainty in an average of 89% of bins across two continuum windows, versus 37% for Attentive CNP with positional encoding. With 5,000 efficiency-model training events and 500 context events, it exceeds CNP and Attentive CNP trained on twice as many events in both peak-core and continuum summaries, and also exceeds context-only KDE and Bernoulli GP. Our method thus balances the reconstruction of sharp physical features with resistance to statistical overfitting, and achieves this with fewer training events.

</details>

Zhu,Lin Zhu,S. H. Zhu,T. J. Zhu,W. D. Zhu,W. J. Zhu,W. Z. Zhu,Y. C. Zhu,Z. A. Zhu,X. Y. Zhuang,M. Zhuge,J. H. Zou,J. Zu*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文利用BESIII实验采集的7.33 fb⁻¹ e⁺e⁻对撞数据，在4.128–4.226 GeV质心能量区间首次观测并研究了Dₛ⁺→f₀(980)μ⁺ν_μ半轻子衰变，测得其绝对分支比为(1.59±0.18±0.11)×10⁻³；结合先前对电子道的测量，验证了轻子味普适性（LFU）；通过单极子形式因子拟合，提取出f⁺f₀(980)(0)|Vcs|=0.500±0.016±0.020，并最终给出迄今最精确的Dₛ→f₀(980)跃迁形式因子f⁺f₀(980)(0)=0.514±0.017±0.021。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验标准模型中的轻子味普适性（LFU），并精确测定Dₛ⁺→f₀(980)ℓ⁺ν_ℓ（ℓ=μ,e）衰变的强子跃迁形式因子，以约束QCD非微扰理论模型（如Lattice QCD、Light-cone sum rules等）。

Method: 基于BESIII在ψ(4160)和ψ(4190)附近采集的7.33 fb⁻¹ e⁺e⁻数据，采用部分重建与全运动学拟合技术识别Dₛ⁺→f₀(980)μ⁺ν_μ（f₀(980)→π⁺π⁻）信号；使用单极子参数化f⁺(q²)=f⁺(0)/(1−q²/mₚₒₗₑ²)拟合q²分布，提取f⁺(0)|Vcs|；结合电子道结果做加权平均，并代入CKMfitter组的|Vcs|值解出f⁺(0)。

Result: 首次观测到Dₛ⁺→f₀(980)μ⁺ν_μ，分支比为(1.59±0.18±0.11)×10⁻³；μ/e分支比为0.92±0.13±0.08，符合LFU；f⁺f₀(980)(0)|Vcs|=0.500±0.016±0.020；最终f⁺f₀(980)(0)=0.514±0.017±0.021，为当前最精确值。

Conclusion: 实验结果支持标准模型轻子味普适性，且提供了Dₛ→f₀(980)强子流-矢量流跃迁形式因子的最精确实验约束，对检验格点QCD、QCD求和规则等理论计算具有关键意义。

Abstract: Using 7.33 fb$^{-1}$ of $e^+e^-$ annihilation data recorded with the BESIII detector at center-of-mass energies from 4.128 to 4.226 GeV, we report the first observation and dynamical study of the semileptonic decay $D^+_s\to f_{0}(980) μ^+ν_μ$. The absolute branching fraction of $D^+_s\to f_{0}(980) μ^+ν_μ$ with $ f_{0}(980)\to π^+ π^-$ is $(1.59 \pm 0.18_{\rm stat} \pm 0.11_{\rm syst}) \times10^{-3}$. Combining this result with our earlier BESIII measurement of ${\mathcal B}(D^+_s\to f_{0}(980) e^+ν_e)$, their ratio is found to be $\frac{{\mathcal B}(D^+_s\to f_{0}(980) μ^+ν_μ)}{{\mathcal B}(D^+_s\to f_{0}(980)e^+ν_e)} = 0.92\pm0.13_{\rm stat}\pm0.08_{\rm syst}$, in agreement with the Standard Model expectation of lepton flavor universality. From a dynamical analysis of the $D_{s}^{+} \to f_{0}(980)μ^+ν_μ$ decay with a simple pole parametrization for the hadronic transition form factor, the product of the form factor $f^{f_{0}(980)}_{+}(0)$ and the $c\to s$ Cabibbo-Kobayashi-Maskawa matrix element $|V_{cs}|$ is determined to be $f^{f_{0}(980)}_{+}(0)|V_{cs}|=0.490\pm0.059_{\rm stat}\pm0.025_{\rm syst}$. Averaging with our previously reported result for the $D_{s}^{+} \to f_{0}(980)e^+ν_e$ decay, we obtain $f^{f_{0}(980)}_{+}(0)|V_{cs}|=0.500\pm0.016_{\rm stat}\pm0.020_{\rm syst}$. Using $|V_{cs}|$ from the CKMfitter group, we extract $f^{f_{0}(980)}_{+}(0)=0.514\pm0.017_{\rm stat}\pm0.021_{\rm syst}$. This represents the most precise determination of the $D_{s} \to f_{0}(980)$ transition form factor to date, and provides stringent tests of various theoretical models.

</details>


### [118] [Comment for the SCAC Facilities Subcommittee from the Division of Particles and Fields of the American Physical Society](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15401)
*D. S. Akerib,A. De Gouvea,P. Elmer,S. Eno,L. Fields,A. Lankford,V. Martinez Outschoorn,P. Meade,H. Murayama*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文是美国物理学会粒子与场分会（DPF）向SCAC设施小组提交的意见书，旨在支持DUNE Phase II探测器及配套设施、以及美国对下一代希格斯工厂的贡献这两项关键项目，依据P5战略规划等社区共识文件阐述其科学必要性与优先级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 响应SCAC设施子小组征询意见，代表美国大学与DOE实验室从事基本粒子与相互作用研究的科学家群体，阐明当前高能物理前沿核心科学问题及设施需求。

Method: 基于P5（Particle Physics Project Prioritization Panel）战略规划及其他社区共识性优先级文档，系统梳理驱动领域发展的关键科学问题，并据此评估两项提议设施项目的科学价值、技术准备度与协同效应。

Result: 明确推荐DUNE Phase II探测器及其支撑基础设施、以及美国参与未来希格斯工厂作为高优先级国家设施投资方向，强调二者在中微子物理、CP破坏、希格斯精确测量及新物理探索中的不可替代作用。

Conclusion: 两项提议项目构成美国高能物理‘发现物理学’战略的核心支柱，符合P5路线图所确立的科学目标与优先次序，应获SCAC设施子小组实质性支持。

Abstract: The Division of Particles and Fields (DPF) of the American Physical Society would like to thank SCAC for providing an opportunity for input to the SCAC facilities subpanel. The DPF represents scientists working at U.S. Universities and DOE Laboratories on understanding fundamental particles and forces. We are focused on Discovery Physics. The subpanel has been asked to consider two proposed DPF-related projects, the DUNE Phase II Detectors and supporting infrastructure and U.S. contributions to a future Higgs Factory. In this note, we briefly describe the questions driving our field and the P5 strategic plan and other community prioritization documents that form the backbone of our comment. The following sections provide short descriptions of the two projects, highlighting information relevant to your charge, answers to your specific questions, and a description of the process that produced this note.

</details>
